- If the p-value is less than 0.01, the function returns "**", which is a strong significance.
- If the p-value is less than 0.05, the function returns "*", which is a moderate significance.
- For all other p-values, the function returns "n.s.", which stands for "not significant".

    (if (string-suffix? string suffix)
        (substring string 0 (- (string-length string) (string-length suffix)))
        string))
